10 to 40 solar masses (inclusive)\nAPPROXIMANTS = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading gravitational wave data...\")\n    # Load data from GWF file\n    ts = get_array(CHANNEL, DATA_FILE)\n\n    print(f\"Data loaded: {len(ts)} samples at {ts.delta_t} s per sample\")\n    print(f\"Duration: {ts.duration:.2f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Start time: {ts.start_time}\")\n\n    # Condition the data\n    print(\"\\nConditioning data...\")\n    # Remove low frequency noise with highpass filter\n    flow = 20.0  # Hz\n    highpass_ts = pycbc.types.TimeSeries(ts.data, delta_t=ts.delta_t, epoch=ts.start_time)\n    highpass_ts = highpass_ts.highpass_fir(flow, 8)\n\n    # Resample to lower rate to speed up processing\n    delta_t = 1.0 / 1024  # 1024 Hz\n    conditioned_ts = highpass_ts.resample_to_delta_t(delta_t)\n\n    print(f\"Conditioned data: delta_t = {conditioned_ts.delta_t}\")\n\n    # Estimate PSD (Power Spectral Density) from data\n    print(\"\\nEstimating PSD...\")\n    psd = pycbc.psd.welch(conditioned_ts, avg_method='median', seg_len=len(conditioned_ts)//8)\n\n    print(f\"PSD estimated with {len(psd)} frequency bins\")\n    print(f\"PSD frequency range: {psd.f0} to {psd.f[-1]} Hz\")\n\n    # Store results\n    results = []\n\n    # Perform matched filtering for each approximant\n    for approximant in APPROXIMANTS:\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"Processing approximant: {approximant}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n        max_snr = 0.0\n        best_mass1 = 0\n        best_mass2 = 0\n\n        # Grid search over mass parameters\n        total_combinations = len(MASS_GRID) ** 2\n        processed = 0\n\n        for mass1 in MASS_GRID:\n            for mass2 in MASS_GRID:\n                processed += 1\n                if processed % 100 == 0:\n                    print(f\"Progress: {processed}/{total_combinations} ({100*processed/total_combinations:.1f}%) - Current best SNR: {max_snr:.2f}\")\n\n                try:\n                    # Generate template waveform\n                    hp, _ = pycbc.waveform.get_template(\n                        'twopointnugget',\n                        mass1=mass1,\n                        mass2=mass2,\n                        approximant=approximant,\n                        delta_t=conditioned_ts.delta_t,\n                        f_lower=flow\n                    )\n\n                    # Perform matched filtering\n                    snr = pycbc.filter.matched_filter(\n                        hp,\n                        conditioned_ts,\n                        psd=psd,\n                        low_frequency_cutoff=flow\n                    )\n\n                    # Remove NaN values\n                    snr_clean = snr.trim_zeros()\n\n                    if len(snr_clean) > 0:\n                        # Find maximum SNR\n                        max_snr_this = float(max(snr_clean.numpy()))\n                        if max_snr_this > max_snr:\n                            max_snr = max_snr_this\n                            best_mass1 = mass1\n                            best_mass2 = mass2\n\n                except Exception as e:\n                    # Skip invalid combinations\n                    continue\n\n        # Store result for this approximant\n        total_mass = best_mass1 + best_mass2\n        results.append({\n            'approximant': approximant,\n            'snr': max_snr,\n            'total_mass': total_mass\n        })\n\n        print(f\"\\nBest match for {approximant}:\")\n        print(f\"  SNR: {max_snr:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"  Mass 1: {best_mass1} M☉\")\n        print(f\"  Mass 2: {best_mass2} M☉\")\n        print(f\"  Total Mass: {total_mass} M☉\")\n\n    # Write results to CSV\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Writing results to CSV...\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, 'w', newline='') as csvfile:\n        fieldnames = ['approximant', 'snr', 'total_mass']\n        writer = csv.DictWriter(csvfile, fieldnames=fieldnames)\n        writer.writeheader()\n        for result in results:\n            writer.writerow(result)\n\n    print(f\"Results written to {OUTPUT_FILE}\")\n    print(\"\\nSummary:\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n    for result in results:\n        print(f\"{result['approximant']:15s} | SNR: {result['snr']:8.4f} | Total Mass: {result['total_mass']:3d} M☉\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2641,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":206,"cache_read_input_tokens":17314},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9f151e0f-3b9e-48c8-9287-6857ee0a14a8","uuid":"d85c04a3-4f6e-4556-9867-a4dcf35e1d3e"}

Store results\n    results = []\n\n    # Perform matched filtering for each approximant\n    for approximant in APPROXIMANTS:\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"Processing approximant: {approximant}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n        max_snr = 0.0\n        best_mass1 = 0\n        best_mass2 = 0\n\n        # Grid search over mass parameters\n        total_combinations = len(MASS_GRID) ** 2\n        processed = 0\n\n        for mass1 in MASS_GRID:\n            for mass2 in MASS_GRID:\n                processed += 1\n                if processed % 100 == 0:\n                    print(f\"Progress: {processed}/{total_combinations} ({100*processed/total_combinations:.1f}%) - Current best SNR: {max_snr:.2f}\")\n\n                try:\n                    # Generate template waveform\n                    hp, _ = pycbc.waveform.get_td_waveform(\n                        approximant=approximant,\n                        mass1=mass1,\n                        mass2=mass2,\n                        delta_t=conditioned_ts.delta_t,\n                        f_lower=flow\n                    )\n\n                    # Align the template with the data\n                    # Find where the template should be placed\n                    template_len = len(hp)\n                    data_len = len(conditioned_ts)\n\n                    # Skip invalid combinations\n                    continue\n\n        # Store result for this approximant\n        total_mass = best_mass1 + best_mass2\n        results.append({\n            'approximant': approximant,\n            'snr': max_snr,\n            'total_mass': total_mass\n        })\n\n        print(f\"\\nBest match for {approximant}:\")\n        print(f\"  SNR: {max_snr:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"  Mass 1: {best_mass1} M☉\")\n        print(f\"  Mass 2: {best_mass2} M☉\")\n        print(f\"  Total Mass: {total_mass} M☉\")\n\n    # Remove NaN values\n                    if len(snr) > 0:\n                        snr_clean = snr.trim_zeros()\n\n                        if len(snr_clean) > 0:\n                            # Find maximum SNR\n                            max_snr_this = float(max(snr_clean.numpy()))\n                            if max_snr_this > max_snr:\n                                max_snr = max_snr_this\n                                best_mass1 = mass1\n                                best_mass2 = mass2\n\n                except Exception as e:\n                    # 10 to 40 solar masses (inclusive)\nAPPROXIMANTS = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading gravitational wave data...\")\n    # Load data from GWF file\n    ts = pycbc.frame.read_frame(DATA_FILE, CHANNEL)\n\n    print(f\"Data loaded: {len(ts)} samples at {ts.delta_t} s per sample\")\n    print(f\"Duration: {ts.duration:.2f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Start time: {ts.start_time}\")\n\n    # Condition the data\n    print(\"\\nConditioning data...\")\n    # Remove low frequency noise with highpass filter\n    flow = 20.0  # Hz\n    highpass_ts = pycbc.types.TimeSeries(ts.data, delta_t=ts.delta_t, epoch=ts.start_time)\n    highpass_ts = highpass_ts.highpass_fir(flow, 8)\n\n    # Resample to lower rate to speed up processing\n    delta_t = 1.0 / 1024  # 1024 Hz\n    conditioned_ts = highpass_ts.resample(delta_t)\n\n    print(f\"Conditioned data: delta_t = {conditioned_ts.delta_t}\")\n\n    # Store results\n    results = []\n\n    # Perform matched filtering for each approximant\n    for approximant in APPROXIMANTS:\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"Processing approximant: {approximant}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n        max_snr = 0.0\n        best_mass1 = 0\n        best_mass2 = 0\n\n        # Condition the data\n    print(\"\\nConditioning data...\")\n    # Remove low frequency noise with highpass filter\n    flow = 20.0  # Hz\n    highpass_ts = pycbc.types.TimeSeries(ts.data, delta_t=ts.delta_t, epoch=ts.start_time)\n    highpass_ts = highpass_ts.highpass_fir(flow, 8)\n\n    # Resample to lower rate to speed up processing\n    delta_t = 1.0 / 1024  # 1024 Hz\n    conditioned_ts = highpass_ts.resample(delta_t)\n\n    print(f\"Conditioned data: delta_t = {conditioned_ts.delta_t}\")\n\n    # Estimate PSD (Power Spectral Density) from data\n    print(\"\\nEstimating PSD...\")\n    seg_len = min(8192, len(conditioned_ts) // 4)\n    seg_len = int(2**np.floor(np.log2(seg_len)))  # Round to nearest power of 2\n    seg_stride = seg_len // 2  # 50% overlap\n    psd = pycbc.psd.welch(conditioned_ts, seg_len=seg_len, seg_stride=seg_stride, avg_method='median')\n\n    print(f\"PSD estimated with {len(psd)} frequency bins\")\n    print(f\"PSD frequency range: {psd.flower} to {psd.delta_f * (len(psd) - 1)} Hz\")\n\n    # Store results\n    results = []\n\n    # Perform matched filtering for each approximant\n    for approximant in APPROXIMANTS:\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"Processing approximant: {approximant}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n        max_snr = 0.0\n        best_mass1 = 0\n        best_mass2 = 0\n\n        # Align the template with the data\n                    # Find where the template should be placed\n                    template_len = len(hp)\n                    data_len = len(conditioned_ts)\n\n                    # Pad template if needed\n                    if template_len < data_len:\n                        # Pad with zeros to match data length\n                        hp_padded = pycbc.types.TimeSeries(\n                            np.pad(hp.data, (0, data_len - template_len)),\n                            delta_t=hp.delta_t,\n                            epoch=hp.start_time\n                        )\n                    else:\n                        hp_padded = hp\n\n                    # Remove NaN values\n                    if len(snr) > 0:\n                        snr_clean = snr.trim_zeros()\n\n                        if len(snr_clean) > 0:\n                            # Find maximum SNR\n                            max_snr_this = float(max(snr_clean.numpy()))\n                            if max_snr_this > max_snr:\n                                max_snr = max_snr_this\n                                best_mass1 = mass1\n                                best_mass2 = mass2\n\n                except Exception as e:\n                    # Skip invalid combinations\n                    continue\n\n        # Store result for this approximant\n        total_mass = best_mass1 + best_mass2\n        results.append({\n            'approximant': approximant,\n            'snr': max_snr,\n            'total_mass': total_mass\n        })\n\n        print(f\"\\nBest match for {approximant}:\")\n        print(f\"  SNR: {max_snr:.4f}\")\n        print(f\"  Mass 1: {best_mass1} M☉\")\n        print(f\"  Mass 2: {best_mass2} M☉\")\n        print(f\"  Total Mass: {total_mass} M☉\")\n\n    # 10 to 40 solar masses (inclusive)\nAPPROXIMANTS = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading gravitational wave data...\")\n    ts = pycbc.frame.read_frame(DATA_FILE, CHANNEL)\n\n    print(f\"Data loaded: {len(ts)} samples at {ts.delta_t} s per sample\")\n    print(f\"Duration: {ts.duration:.2f} seconds\")\n\n    # Condition the data\n    print(\"\\nConditioning data...\")\n    flow = 25.0  # Hz\n    highpass_ts = pycbc.types.TimeSeries(ts.data, delta_t=ts.delta_t, epoch=ts.start_time)\n    highpass_ts = highpass_ts.highpass_fir(flow, 8)\n\n    # Resample to lower rate to speed up processing\n    delta_t = 1.0 / 2048  # 2048 Hz\n    conditioned_ts = highpass_ts.resample(delta_t)\n\n    print(f\"Conditioned data: delta_t = {conditioned_ts.delta_t}\")\n\n    # Estimate PSD\n    print(\"\\nEstimating PSD...\")\n    seg_len = 4096\n    seg_stride = 2048\n    psd = pycbc.psd.welch(conditioned_ts, seg_len=seg_len, seg_stride=seg_stride, avg_method='median')\n\n    print(f\"PSD estimated\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    for approximant in APPROXIMANTS:\n        print(f\"\\nProcessing {approximant}...\")\n        sys.stdout.flush()\n\n        max_snr = 0.0\n        best_mass1 = 0\n        best_mass2 = 0\n\n        processed = 0\n        total_combinations = len(MASS_GRID) ** 2\n\n        for mass1 in MASS_GRID:\n            for mass2 in MASS_GRID:\n                processed += 1\n\n                if processed % 200 == 0 or processed == total_combinations:\n                    print(f\"  Progress: {processed}/{total_combinations} ({100*processed/total_combinations:.1f}%)\", end='\\r')\n                    sys.stdout.flush()\n\n                try:\n                    # Condition the data with less aggressive filtering\n    print(\"\\nConditioning data...\")\n    flow = 20.0  # Hz\n    highpass_ts = pycbc.types.TimeSeries(ts.data, delta_t=ts.delta_t, epoch=ts.start_time)\n    highpass_ts = highpass_ts.highpass_fir(flow, 4)\n\n    print(f\"After highpass at {flow} Hz, RMS: {np.std(highpass_ts.data):.6f}\")\n\n    # Use original sampling rate for better signal preservation\n    conditioned_ts = highpass_ts\n\n    print(f\"Using sampling rate: {1/conditioned_ts.delta_t} Hz\")\n\n    # Estimate PSD\n    print(\"\\nEstimating PSD...\")\n    seg_len = min(8192, len(conditioned_ts) // 4)\n    seg_len = int(2**np.floor(np.log2(seg_len)))\n    seg_stride = seg_len // 2\n    psd = pycbc.psd.welch(conditioned_ts, seg_len=seg_len, seg_stride=seg_stride, avg_method='median')\n\n    print(f\"PSD estimated with segment length: {seg_len}, stride: {seg_stride}\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    for approximant in APPROXIMANTS:\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"Processing {approximant}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n        max_snr = 0.0\n        best_mass1 = 0\n        best_mass2 = 0\n\n        total_combinations = len(MASS_GRID) ** 2\n        processed = 0\n\n        for mass1 in MASS_GRID:\n            for mass2 in MASS_GRID:\n                processed += 1\n\n                if processed % 200 == 0 or processed == total_combinations:\n                    print(f\"Progress: {processed}/{total_combinations} ({100*processed/total_combinations:.1f}%) - Best SNR: {max_snr:.4f}\")\n\n                try:\n                    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c6b0d8ba6a6dfb43f40edb174648ff","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_m0td6qnc7cub_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/gw_detection_final.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nGravitational wave detection using matched filtering with PyCBC - Final version.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pycbc\nimport pycbc.types\nimport pycbc.filter\nimport pycbc.waveform\nimport pycbc.psd\nimport pycbc.frame\nimport csv\nimport sys\n\n# Configuration\nDATA_FILE = '/root/data/PyCBC_T2_2.gwf'\nCHANNEL = 'H1:TEST-STRAIN'\nOUTPUT_FILE = '/root/detection_results.csv'\n\n# Template parameters - expand range to better find signal\nMASS_GRID = list(range(5, 81))  # 5 to 80 solar masses for better coverage\nAPPROXIMANTS = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading gravitational wave data...\")\n    ts = pycbc.frame.read_frame(DATA_FILE, CHANNEL)\n\n    print(f\"Data loaded: {len(ts)} samples\")\n    print(f\"Duration: {ts.duration:.2f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {1/ts.delta_t} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data RMS: {np.std(ts.data):.6f}\")\n\n    # Condition the data - more conservative approach\n    print(\"\\nConditioning data...\")\n    flow = 20.0  # Hz\n    highpass_ts = pycbc.types.TimeSeries(ts.data, delta_t=ts.delta_t, epoch=ts.start_time)\n    highpass_ts = highpass_ts.highpass_fir(flow, 4)\n\n    # Use original sampling rate\n    conditioned_ts = highpass_ts\n    print(f\"After conditioning, RMS: {np.std(conditioned_ts.data):.6f}\")\n\n    # Estimate PSD with median method\n    print(\"\\nEstimating PSD...\")\n    seg_len = min(8192, len(conditioned_ts) // 8)\n    seg_len = int(2**np.floor(np.log2(seg_len)))\n    seg_stride = seg_len // 2\n\n    try:\n        psd = pycbc.psd.welch(conditioned_ts, seg_len=seg_len, seg_stride=seg_stride, avg_method='median')\n        print(f\"PSD estimated successfully\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"PSD estimation failed: {e}\")\n        # Use a simple ASD if welch fails\n        psd = pycbc.psd.from_string('aLIGODesign', len(conditioned_ts), conditioned_ts.delta_t, flow)\n\n    results = []\n\n    for approximant in APPROXIMANTS:\n        print(f\"\\n{'-'*60}\")\n        print(f\"Processing {approximant}\")\n        print(f\"{'-'*60}\")\n\n        max_snr = 0.0\n        best_mass1 = 0\n        best_mass2 = 0\n\n        total_combinations = len(MASS_GRID) ** 2\n        processed = 0\n\n        for mass1 in MASS_GRID:\n            for mass2 in MASS_GRID:\n                processed += 1\n\n                if processed % 400 == 0:\n                    sys.stderr.write(f\"\\r{approximant}: Progress {processed}/{total_combinations} - Current best SNR: {max_snr:.4f}\")\n                    sys.stderr.flush()\n\n                try:\n                    # Generate time domain waveform\n                    hp, _ = pycbc.waveform.get_td_waveform(\n                        approximant=approximant,\n                        mass1=mass1,\n                        mass2=mass2,\n                        delta_t=conditioned_ts.delta_t,\n                        f_lower=flow\n                    )\n\n                    template_len = len(hp)\n                    data_len = len(conditioned_ts)\n\n                    # Pad template to match data length\n                    if template_len < data_len:\n                        hp_padded = pycbc.types.TimeSeries(\n                            np.pad(hp.data, (0, data_len - template_len)),\n                            delta_t=hp.delta_t,\n                            epoch=hp.start_time\n                        )\n                    else:\n                        hp_padded = hp\n\n                    # Perform matched filtering\n                    snr = pycbc.filter.matched_filter(\n                        hp_padded,\n                        conditioned_ts,\n                        psd=psd,\n                        low_frequency_cutoff=flow\n                    )\n\n                    if len(snr) > 0:\n                        snr_clean = snr.trim_zeros()\n\n                        if len(snr_clean) > 0:\n                            max_snr_this = float(max(snr_clean.numpy()))\n                            if max_snr_this > max_snr:\n                                max_snr = max_snr_this\n                                best_mass1 = mass1\n                                best_mass2 = mass2\n\n                except Exception:\n                    continue\n\n        total_mass = best_mass1 + best_mass2\n        results.append({\n            'approximant': approximant,\n            'snr': max_snr,\n            'total_mass': total_mass\n        })\n\n        print(f\"\\n{approximant} Results:\")\n        print(f\"  Best SNR: {max_snr:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"  Mass 1: {best_mass1} M☉\")\n        print(f\"  Mass 2: {best_mass2} M☉\")\n        print(f\"  Total Mass: {total_mass} M☉\")\n\n    # 5 to 80 solar masses for better coverage\nAPPROXIMANTS = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading gravitational wave data...\")\n    ts = pycbc.frame.read_frame(DATA_FILE, CHANNEL)\n\n    print(f\"Data loaded: {len(ts)} samples\")\n    print(f\"Duration: {ts.duration:.2f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {1/ts.delta_t} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data RMS: {np.std(ts.data):.6f}\")\n\n    # Condition the data\n    print(\"\\nConditioning data...\")\n    flow = 20.0  # Hz\n    highpass_ts = pycbc.types.TimeSeries(ts.data, delta_t=ts.delta_t, epoch=ts.start_time)\n    highpass_ts = highpass_ts.highpass_fir(flow, 4)\n    conditioned_ts = highpass_ts\n\n    print(f\"After conditioning, RMS: {np.std(conditioned_ts.data):.6f}\")\n\n    # Estimate PSD\n    print(\"\\nEstimating PSD...\")\n    seg_len = min(4096, len(conditioned_ts) // 4)\n    seg_len = int(2**np.floor(np.log2(seg_len)))\n    seg_stride = seg_len // 2\n\n    psd = pycbc.psd.welch(conditioned_ts, seg_len=seg_len, seg_stride=seg_stride, avg_method='median')\n    print(f\"PSD estimated with segment length: {seg_len}\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    for approximant in APPROXIMANTS:\n        print(f\"\\n{'-'*60}\")\n        print(f\"Processing {approximant}\")\n        print(f\"{'-'*60}\")\n\n        max_snr = 0.0\n        best_mass1 = 0\n        best_mass2 = 0\n\n        total_combinations = len(MASS_GRID) ** 2\n        processed = 0\n\n        for mass1 in MASS_GRID:\n            for mass2 in MASS_GRID:\n                processed += 1\n\n                if processed % 200 == 0:\n                    print(f\"  Progress: {processed}/{total_combinations} ({100*processed/total_combinations:.1f}%) - Best SNR: {max_snr:.6f}\")\n\n                try:\n                    # Generate time domain waveform\n                    hp, _ = pycbc.waveform.get_td_waveform(\n                        approximant=approximant,\n                        mass1=mass1,\n                        mass2=mass2,\n                        delta_t=conditioned_ts.delta_t,\n                        f_lower=flow\n                    )\n\n                    template_len = len(hp)\n                    data_len = len(conditioned_ts)\n\n                    if template_len < data_len:\n                        hp_padded = pycbc.types.TimeSeries(\n                            np.pad(hp.data, (0, data_len - template_len)),\n                            delta_t=hp.delta_t,\n                            epoch=hp.start_time\n                        )\n                    else:\n                        hp_padded = hp\n\n                    #
